# Component recipes (generated)

GENERATED by `tool/recipes/run` from raft-ui 0.7.0 (`dist/index.mjs` sha256 `83bf0bff3509`) with tailwindcss 4.2.2. Do not edit.

Tailwind input CSS: `@import "tailwindcss"; @import "./styles.css"; @import "raft-web-theme.css";` (`raft-web-theme.css` = the Web client's `@theme` overrides, `tool/recipes/web-theme.css`).

Every `tv()` recipe is evaluated with the real tailwind-variants + raft-ui `cn` (tailwind-merge) for every variant
combination, and every resulting class is compiled by Tailwind v4 with raft-ui `styles.css` as input. Values below are
after cascade and `var()` resolution: lengths in logical px, `token(x)` = foundation token `--x` in lowerCamelCase
(resolved per theme at runtime), literal colours as `#AARRGGBB`. State rows show only properties that change.
Authoritative data: `build/recipes/recipes.json`; Dart: `packages/raft_theme/lib/recipes/`.

- Distinct classes in recipe sources: 1944

Classes for which Tailwind emits no CSS (inert on the Web as well):

- `blur-0` (drawerMotion): Tailwind v4 has no `blur-0` (v4 spells it `blur-none`).
- `data-[state=copied]:[&_[data-icon=check]]:blur-0` (copyableCode): Wraps `blur-0`, which is not a v4 utility.
- `data-[state=idle]:[&_[data-icon=copy]]:blur-0` (copyableCode): Wraps `blur-0`, which is not a v4 utility.
- `scrollbar-none` (composerAttachment, tabs): No `scrollbar-*` utility/plugin in raft-ui or the Web build.
